import * as React from "react";
import { DrillSplit } from '@/components/drill/DrillSplit';
import { Check, Eye, EyeOff, Flag, X } from "lucide-react";
import { HighlightedText } from "@/components/drill/HighlightedText";
import InlineChoiceExplanation, {
  type InlineChoiceNotes,
} from "@/components/drill/official/InlineChoiceExplanation";
import {
  choiceSection,
  type Highlight,
  type HighlightMode,
  type HighlightRegion,
} from "@/lib/highlightUtils";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { useDeferredPick } from "@/hooks/useDeferredPick";

interface Question {
  qid: string;
  qnum?: number | string;
  stimulus: string;
  questionStem: string;
  answerChoices: Record<string, string>;
}

interface Props {
  question: Question;
  displayNumber: number;
  selectedAnswer: string;
  eliminatedAnswers: Set<string>;
  isFlagged: boolean;
  answerRevealed?: boolean;
  correctAnswer?: string;
  /**
   * Per-choice explanation content, rendered inline beneath each choice once
   * the answer is revealed. Omit it entirely (section mode, or before reveal)
   * and no inline layer renders at all.
   */
  inlineNotes?: InlineChoiceNotes | null;
  highlights: Highlight[];
  highlightMode: HighlightMode;
  searchTerm?: string;
  fontScale?: number;
  onAnswerSelect: (key: string) => void;
  onEliminate: (key: string) => void;
  onToggleFlag: () => void;
  /**
   * Called on every mouse-up in the pane with every highlightable block on
   * screen: the stimulus, the stem, and each answer choice. The handler
   * clips the live selection to each block, so one drag can mark several.
   * Returns true when it consumed the selection as a highlight.
   */
  onTextSelection: (regions: HighlightRegion[]) => boolean | void;
  onHighlightClick: (highlightId: string) => void;
  /**
   * Rendered between the stem and the choices. Blind review puts its Actual/BR
   * tabs here so it can run inside this exact pane rather than reimplementing
   * the exam screen a second time in a different visual language.
   */
  aboveChoices?: React.ReactNode;
}

function stripDataArtifacts(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/\s*\{#\d+\}\s*/g, " ")
    .replace(/[ \t]{2,}/g, " ")
    .replace(/\n[ \t]+/g, "\n")
    .trim();
}

function cleanAnswerChoice(key: string, text: string): string {
  const escapedKey = key.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&");
  return stripDataArtifacts(text)
    .replace(new RegExp(`^\\s*[-–—]?\\s*\\[${escapedKey}\\]\\s*`, "i"), "")
    .replace(new RegExp(`^\\s*[-–—]?\\s*\\(${escapedKey}\\)\\s*`, "i"), "")
    .replace(new RegExp(`^\\s*[-–—]?\\s*${escapedKey}[.)]\\s*`, "i"), "")
    .trim();
}

export default function OfficialQuestionPane({
  question,
  displayNumber,
  selectedAnswer,
  eliminatedAnswers,
  isFlagged,
  answerRevealed = false,
  correctAnswer,
  inlineNotes,
  highlights,
  highlightMode,
  searchTerm,
  fontScale = 1,
  onAnswerSelect,
  onEliminate,
  onToggleFlag,
  onTextSelection,
  onHighlightClick,
  aboveChoices,
}: Props) {
  const stimulusHighlights = highlights.filter((h) => h.section === "stimulus");
  const stemHighlights = highlights.filter((h) => h.section === "stem");
  const stimulusText = stripDataArtifacts(question.stimulus);
  const stemText = stripDataArtifacts(question.questionStem);
  const answerEntries = Object.entries(question.answerChoices).map(([key, text]) => [
    key,
    cleanAnswerChoice(key, text),
  ] as const);
  const highlightsForChoice = (key: string) =>
    highlights.filter((h) => h.section === choiceSection(key));

  const stimulusRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const stemRef = React.useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);
  const choiceRefs = React.useRef(new Map<string, HTMLDivElement>());
  // Set on mouse-up when the drag was a marking gesture, read by the click
  // that follows it. A drag that ends on a choice must not also pick it.
  const markedRef = React.useRef(false);
  // Under a marker tool a pick waits a beat, so the first press of a
  // double-click on a word (which is marking, not answering) can be called
  // off by the second. With no tool active a click picks at once.
  const deferredPick = useDeferredPick();

  const handleMouseUp = () => {
    const selection = window.getSelection();
    const dragged = !!selection && !selection.isCollapsed;
    const regions: HighlightRegion[] = [];
    if (stimulusRef.current) {
      regions.push({ section: "stimulus", container: stimulusRef.current, source: stimulusText });
    }
    if (stemRef.current) {
      regions.push({ section: "stem", container: stemRef.current, source: stemText });
    }
    for (const [key, text] of answerEntries) {
      const el = choiceRefs.current.get(key);
      if (el) regions.push({ section: choiceSection(key), container: el, source: text });
    }
    const marked = onTextSelection(regions);
    // With no tool active the selection is left alone; it still must not
    // read as a click on the choice it happened to end on.
    markedRef.current = dragged || !!marked;
    if (markedRef.current) deferredPick.cancel();
  };

  // 7Sage sets both the stimulus and the answer choices at 19px and the
  // student reads ours next to theirs, so the two match rather than land a
  // couple of pixels apart. The stimulus carries the extra weight of the serif
  // face; stem and choices stay on the sans stack at the same size.
  const baseSize = 19 * fontScale;
  const officialContentTextStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    color: "#111827",
    fontFamily: '"Helvetica Neue", Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif',
    fontSize: `${baseSize}px`,
    fontWeight: 400,
    lineHeight: 1.6,
  };
  const stimStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
    ...officialContentTextStyle,
    fontFamily: "Fraunces, ui-serif, Georgia, serif",
    fontSize: `${19 * fontScale}px`,
    lineHeight: 1.7,
    // Fraunces is a variable font with an optical-size axis. Left on `auto` the
    // browser picks the cut from the rendered px size, which drifts as the
    // student changes fontScale. Pin it to the text-optimised end so hairlines
    // stay the same weight at every scale.
    fontOpticalSizing: "none",
    fontVariationSettings: '"opsz" 14',
  };
  const stemStyle: React.CSSProperties = officialContentTextStyle;
  // The answer-choice text and letter badges used Tailwind's `text-sm`/`text-xs`,
  // which are hard px values and silently overrode the inherited scaled size —
  // so fontScale grew the stimulus and stem while the answers stayed at 14px.
  const choiceTextStyle: React.CSSProperties = { fontSize: `${baseSize}px` };
  const choiceBadgeStyle: React.CSSProperties = { fontSize: `${baseSize * 0.78}px` };

  return (
    <div
      className="relative flex flex-col flex-1 min-h-0 bg-white text-slate-950"
      onMouseUp={handleMouseUp}
    >
      <DrillSplit
        ratio="passage-weighted"
        maxWidthClassName={null}
        left={
          // Stimulus column
          <div
            className={cn(
              "min-w-0 px-6 pt-6 pb-7 sm:pl-9 sm:pr-8 lg:border-r border-slate-200 whitespace-pre-wrap overflow-y-auto",
              // Cap the reading measure. The frame is max-w-[1400px] and this column
              // takes 53fr of it, which ran ~100 characters per line — well past the
              // 45-75 readable band. `ch` is the width of "0", which is narrow in
              // Fraunces, so 52ch measures out to ~68 real characters.
              "[&>*]:max-w-[52ch]",
              // Fraunces is high-contrast; grayscale smoothing thins its hairlines.
              "subpixel-antialiased",
              "selection:bg-blue-100 selection:text-slate-950",
              "[&_p]:mb-4 [&_p:last-child]:mb-0",
              "bg-white",
            )}
            style={stimStyle}
            ref={stimulusRef}
          >
            <HighlightedText
              text={stimulusText}
              highlights={stimulusHighlights}
              onHighlightClick={onHighlightClick}
              eraserMode={highlightMode === "erase"}
              searchTerm={searchTerm}
            />
          </div>
        }
        right={
          // Question column
          <div className="min-w-0 px-6 py-7 sm:pl-10 sm:pr-8 bg-white overflow-y-auto" style={stemStyle}>
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div className="flex-1 flex items-start gap-2">
                <span className="font-bold tabular-nums shrink-0 min-w-[20px] text-slate-950">
                  {displayNumber}.
                </span>
                <div className="flex-1 text-slate-950" ref={stemRef}>
                  <HighlightedText
                    text={stemText}
                    highlights={stemHighlights}
                    onHighlightClick={onHighlightClick}
                    eraserMode={highlightMode === "erase"}
                    searchTerm={searchTerm}
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onToggleFlag}
                aria-pressed={isFlagged}
                aria-label={isFlagged ? "Remove flag" : "Flag question"}
                title={isFlagged ? "Remove flag" : "Flag question"}
                className={cn(
                  "shrink-0 p-1.5 rounded transition-colors",
                  isFlagged ? "bg-amber-100" : "hover:bg-slate-100",
                )}
              >
                <Flag
                  className={cn(
                    "w-[24px] h-[24px] transition-colors text-slate-950",
                    isFlagged && "fill-amber-400",
                  )}
                  strokeWidth={2}
                />
              </button>
            </div>

            {aboveChoices && <div className="mt-4">{aboveChoices}</div>}

            <ul className="mt-4 space-y-3.5">
              {answerEntries.map(([key, text]) => {
                const isSelected = selectedAnswer === key;
                const isEliminated = eliminatedAnswers.has(key);
                const isCorrect = answerRevealed && correctAnswer === key;
                // The choice they committed to, once it is known to be wrong.
                // Without this a wrong answer stayed the same neutral grey as an
                // unrevealed selection, so revealing said what was right without
                // ever saying what they did.
                const isWrongPick = answerRevealed && isSelected && correctAnswer !== key;
                // The inline layer only ever appears once the answer is revealed.
                const inline =
                  answerRevealed && inlineNotes ? inlineNotes[key] ?? {} : undefined;
                const locked = isEliminated || answerRevealed;
                const choiceTextId = `choice-${question.qid}-${key}`;

                return (
                  <li
                    key={key}
                    className={cn(
                      "group relative rounded-lg border transition-all duration-200",
                      "grid grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto] items-start gap-3 p-3",
                      isCorrect
                        ? "border-green-500 bg-green-50"
                        : isWrongPick
                        ? "border-red-500 bg-red-50"
                        : isSelected
                        ? "border-slate-500 bg-slate-100 shadow-sm"
                        : isEliminated
                        ? "border-transparent bg-white opacity-55 grayscale-[0.5]"
                        : "border-transparent bg-white hover:border-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-50",
                    )}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => !locked && onAnswerSelect(key)}
                      disabled={locked}
                      aria-describedby={choiceTextId}
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border font-semibold transition-all",
                        isCorrect
                          ? "border-green-600 bg-green-500 text-white"
                          : isWrongPick
                          ? "border-red-600 bg-red-500 text-white"
                          : isSelected
                          ? "border-slate-900 bg-slate-900 text-white"
                          : "border-transparent text-slate-950 group-hover:border-slate-300",
                      )}
                      style={choiceBadgeStyle}
                    >
                      {key}
                    </button>
                    {/* The text is not a button: a browser will not start a
                        drag-selection inside one, which is what kept the
                        highlighter off the answer choices. A plain click here
                        still picks the choice; a drag that produced a
                        selection never does. The letter badge stays the
                        keyboard control and describes itself with this text. */}
                    <div
                      id={choiceTextId}
                      ref={(el) => {
                        if (el) choiceRefs.current.set(key, el);
                        else choiceRefs.current.delete(key);
                      }}
                      onClick={(event) => {
                        if (markedRef.current) {
                          markedRef.current = false;
                          return;
                        }
                        if (locked) return;
                        // A click on a highlight is aimed at the highlight
                        // (the eraser has just taken it), not at the answer.
                        const onHighlight = !!(event.target as HTMLElement).closest?.(
                          "[data-highlight-id]",
                        );
                        if (onHighlight && highlightMode === "erase") return;
                        if (highlightMode === "none" || highlightMode === "erase") {
                          onAnswerSelect(key);
                          return;
                        }
                        deferredPick.schedule(() => {
                          // The second press of a double-click marked a word
                          // in the meantime; that was never a pick.
                          if (markedRef.current) return;
                          onAnswerSelect(key);
                        });
                      }}
                      onDoubleClick={() => deferredPick.cancel()}
                      className={cn(
                        "text-left leading-relaxed transition-colors",
                        !locked && "cursor-pointer",
                        isCorrect
                          ? "text-green-900"
                          : isWrongPick
                          ? "text-red-900"
                          : isSelected
                          ? "text-slate-950"
                          : "text-slate-950 group-hover:text-slate-950",
                      )}
                      style={choiceTextStyle}
                    >
                      <HighlightedText
                        text={text}
                        highlights={highlightsForChoice(key)}
                        onHighlightClick={onHighlightClick}
                        eraserMode={highlightMode === "erase"}
                        searchTerm={searchTerm}
                        paragraphStyle={{ margin: 0, lineHeight: "inherit" }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    {answerRevealed && (isCorrect || isWrongPick) ? (
                      // Colour alone must never carry the verdict (ADR 0001), and
                      // once the answer is out the cross-out control is dead
                      // anyway, so its corner becomes the icon and the label.
                      <span
                        role="img"
                        aria-label={isCorrect ? "Correct answer" : "Your answer, incorrect"}
                        title={isCorrect ? "Correct answer" : "Your answer, incorrect"}
                        className={cn(
                          "mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center",
                          isCorrect ? "text-green-700" : "text-red-700",
                        )}
                      >
                        {isCorrect ? <Check size={18} /> : <X size={18} />}
                      </span>
                    ) : (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={(event) => {
                        event.stopPropagation();
                        if (!answerRevealed) onEliminate(key);
                      }}
                      disabled={answerRevealed}
                      // A screen reader announced this as an unlabelled button.
                      // RC's equivalent has carried both of these since it was
                      // written; this is LR catching up to it.
                      aria-label={
                        isEliminated ? `Restore choice ${key}` : `Cross out choice ${key}`
                      }
                      title={isEliminated ? "Restore" : "Cross out"}
                      className={cn(
                        "mt-0.5 p-1 transition-opacity",
                        isEliminated ? "text-slate-700" : "text-slate-400 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:text-slate-800",
                      )}
                    >
                      {isEliminated ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
                    </button>
                    )}
                    {inline && (
                      <div className="col-start-2 col-end-4">
                        <InlineChoiceExplanation
                          letter={key}
                          isCorrect={isCorrect}
                          isSelected={isSelected}
                          content={inline}
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </div>
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}

import * as React from "react";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import type { LrExplanationData, LrStyleKey } from "@/hooks/useLrExplanation";

/**
 * The drawer's Explanation tab: everything about the question as a whole.
 * Per-choice content lives inline under the choices themselves
 * (`InlineChoiceExplanation`), not here.
 *
 * Two rules survive from the retired `ExplanationPanel`:
 *  - The style selector renders only when the source published more than one
 *    style for this question (~5% of the bank).
 *  - Where a non-standard style carries no steps, the source said "(same as
 *    Standard)" and did not repeat the text. Rendering nothing would
 *    misrepresent that, so Standard's breakdown is borrowed and labelled. The
 *    absence only ever means "identical to Standard", never "none exists".
 */

export interface DrillExplanationContext {
  data?: LrExplanationData | null;
  isLoading?: boolean;
  isError?: boolean;
  /** Nothing is shown until the answer is revealed. */
  revealed: boolean;
  /**
   * A sealed sitting never reveals mid-section. Say so, rather than promising
   * an unlock that cannot happen until the section is scored.
   */
  sealed?: boolean;
  /** Shared with the inline layer, so switching style swaps both surfaces. */
  activeStyle: LrStyleKey | null;
  onStyleChange: (style: LrStyleKey) => void;
  /** Hand-off to the AI tutor when the source published no prose. */
  onAskTutor?: () => void;
}

export default function DrillExplanationTab({
  data,
  isLoading,
  isError,
  revealed,
  sealed,
  activeStyle,
  onStyleChange,
  onAskTutor,
}: DrillExplanationContext) {
  const styles = data?.styles ?? [];
  const current = styles.find((s) => s.style === activeStyle) ?? styles[0];

  const standardSteps = styles.find((s) => s.style === "standard")?.steps ?? [];
  const borrowsStandard =
    current != null &&
    current.style !== "standard" &&
    current.steps.length === 0 &&
    standardSteps.length > 0;
  const steps = borrowsStandard ? standardSteps : current?.steps ?? [];

  if (!revealed) {
    return (
      <Body>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-slate-400">
          {sealed
            ? 'Explanations open after the section is scored.'
            : 'The explanation unlocks once you have answered or revealed this question.'}
        </p>
      </Body>
    );
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <Body>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-slate-400">Loading explanation…</p>
      </Body>
    );
  }

  if (isError) {
    return (
      <Body>
        <p className="text-[13px] text-slate-400">
          Could not load the explanation. Try again in a moment.
        </p>
      </Body>
    );
  }

  if (!data?.hasAuthored) {
    return (
      <Body>
        <p className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500">
          No written explanation was published for this question.
        </p>
        {onAskTutor && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onAskTutor}
            className="self-start rounded-full border border-slate-400 bg-white px-4 py-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-slate-700 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100"
          >
            Ask Joshua about this question
          </button>
        )}
      </Body>
    );
  }

  return (
    <Body>
      {data.correctChoice && (
        <p className="text-[13px] text-slate-600">
          Correct answer:{" "}
          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{data.correctChoice}</span>
        </p>
      )}

      {styles.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {styles.map((s) => {
            const active = current != null && s.style === current.style;
            return (
              <button
                key={s.style}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onStyleChange(s.style)}
                aria-pressed={active}
                className={cn(
                  "rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors",
                  active
                    ? "bg-slate-900 text-white"
                    : "border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100",
                )}
              >
                {s.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}

      {steps.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
          <h3 className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
            Argument breakdown
            {borrowsStandard && (
              <span className="ml-2 normal-case tracking-normal text-slate-400">
                same as Standard
              </span>
            )}
          </h3>
          {steps.map((step) => (
            <div key={step.position} className="text-[13px] leading-relaxed">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{step.label}: </span>
              <span className="whitespace-pre-line text-slate-600">{step.body}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <p className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-[12px] italic leading-relaxed text-slate-400">
        Why each choice is right or wrong sits under that choice, beside the question.
      </p>
    </Body>
  );
}

function Body({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-1 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto border-t border-slate-100 px-5 py-4">
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { supabase } from '@/integrations/supabase/client';

/**
 * Authored LSAT Lab explanations, read from the `lr_*` tables.
 *
 * These are transcribed verbatim from the source and verified against LSAT
 * Lab's own API (see `ai-os/wiki/explanations/COVERAGE-AUDIT.md`). Nothing here
 * is generated: if a field is null the source did not provide it, and the UI
 * must render that absence honestly rather than filling it in.
 *
 * Coverage is deliberately partial. Roughly 350 of the ~3,770 ingested
 * questions have no authored prose at all — LSAT Lab published only a video
 * for them. `hasAuthored` is the flag callers should branch on; that is the
 * signal for falling back to the AI tutor.
 */

export type LrStyleKey = 'single' | 'standard' | 'brief' | 'another_way';

export const LR_STYLE_LABELS: Record<LrStyleKey, string> = {
  single: 'Explanation',
  standard: 'Standard',
  brief: 'Brief',
  another_way: 'Another Way',
};

/** Display order for the style selector; mirrors LSAT Lab's own ordering. */
const STYLE_ORDER: LrStyleKey[] = ['standard', 'brief', 'another_way', 'single'];

export interface LrChoice {
  letter: string;
  text: string;
  /** Wrong-answer type, e.g. "Out of Scope". Null when the source had none. */
  tag: string | null;
  /** Percent of students who picked this choice. Null when not published. */
  studentPercent: number | null;
  isCorrect: boolean;
}

export interface LrStep {
  position: number;
  label: string;
  body: string;
}

export interface LrStyle {
  style: LrStyleKey;
  label: string;
  /** Ordered argument-breakdown steps. Empty when the source had none. */
  steps: LrStep[];
  /** Per-choice prose, keyed by letter. A letter may legitimately be absent. */
  notesByLetter: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface LrExplanationData {
  questionId: string;
  preptest: number;
  section: number;
  questionNumber: number;
  questionType: string;
  correctChoice: string | null;
  choices: LrChoice[];
  styles: LrStyle[];
  /**
   * True when at least one style carries real prose — a breakdown step or a
   * per-choice note. Tags and percentages alone do not count: a question can
   * have every choice tagged and still have no authored explanation.
   */
  hasAuthored: boolean;
}

export interface LrExplanationQuery {
  pt?: number | null;
  section?: number | null;
  questionNumber?: number | null;
}

function toStyleKey(value: string): LrStyleKey {
  return (STYLE_ORDER as string[]).includes(value) ? (value as LrStyleKey) : 'single';
}

export async function fetchLrExplanation({
  pt,
  section,
  questionNumber,
}: LrExplanationQuery): Promise<LrExplanationData | null> {
  if (pt == null || section == null || questionNumber == null) return null;

  const { data: question, error: qErr } = await supabase
    .from('lr_questions')
    .select('id, preptest, section, question_number, question_type, correct_choice')
    .eq('preptest', pt)
    .eq('section', section)
    .eq('question_number', questionNumber)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (qErr) throw qErr;
  if (!question) return null;

  const [choicesRes, explRes] = await Promise.all([
    supabase
      .from('lr_answer_choices')
      .select('letter, choice_text, tag, student_percent, is_correct')
      .eq('question_id', question.id)
      .order('letter'),
    supabase
      .from('lr_explanations')
      .select('id, style')
      .eq('question_id', question.id),
  ]);

  if (choicesRes.error) throw choicesRes.error;
  if (explRes.error) throw explRes.error;

  const explanationRows = explRes.data ?? [];
  const explanationIds = explanationRows.map((row) => row.id);

  // Steps and notes are fetched for every style at once, then grouped, so the
  // style selector switches without another round trip.
  const [stepsRes, notesRes] = explanationIds.length
    ? await Promise.all([
        supabase
          .from('lr_explanation_steps')
          .select('explanation_id, position, label, body')
          .in('explanation_id', explanationIds)
          .order('position'),
        supabase
          .from('lr_explanation_choice_notes')
          .select('explanation_id, letter, body')
          .in('explanation_id', explanationIds),
      ])
    : [{ data: [], error: null }, { data: [], error: null }];

  if (stepsRes.error) throw stepsRes.error;
  if (notesRes.error) throw notesRes.error;

  const styles: LrStyle[] = explanationRows
    .map((row) => {
      const key = toStyleKey(row.style);
      const steps = (stepsRes.data ?? [])
        .filter((s) => s.explanation_id === row.id)
        .map((s) => ({ position: s.position, label: s.label, body: s.body }))
        .sort((a, b) => a.position - b.position);
      const notesByLetter: Record<string, string> = {};
      for (const note of notesRes.data ?? []) {
        if (note.explanation_id === row.id) notesByLetter[note.letter] = note.body;
      }
      return { style: key, label: LR_STYLE_LABELS[key], steps, notesByLetter };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => STYLE_ORDER.indexOf(a.style) - STYLE_ORDER.indexOf(b.style));

  const choices: LrChoice[] = (choicesRes.data ?? []).map((c) => ({
    letter: c.letter,
    text: c.choice_text,
    tag: c.tag,
    studentPercent: c.student_percent,
    isCorrect: c.is_correct,
  }));

  const hasAuthored = styles.some(
    (s) => s.steps.length > 0 || Object.keys(s.notesByLetter).length > 0,
  );

  return {
    questionId: question.id,
    preptest: question.preptest,
    section: question.section,
    questionNumber: question.question_number,
    questionType: question.question_type,
    correctChoice: question.correct_choice,
    choices,
    styles,
    hasAuthored,
  };
}

export function useLrExplanation(query: LrExplanationQuery, enabled = true) {
  const { pt, section, questionNumber } = query;
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['lr-explanation', pt, section, questionNumber],
    queryFn: () => fetchLrExplanation(query),
    enabled: enabled && pt != null && section != null && questionNumber != null,
    staleTime: Infinity, // authored content is immutable between ingests
  });
}

import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import OfficialQuestionPane from "./OfficialQuestionPane";
import { choiceSection, type Highlight } from "@/lib/highlightUtils";

/** The element that carries a choice's colour classes: the text block itself. */
function choiceText(name: RegExp): HTMLElement {
  const el = screen.getByText(name).closest("[id^='choice-']");
  if (!(el instanceof HTMLElement)) throw new Error(`no choice block for ${name}`);
  return el;
}

/** Fake a live selection of `text` so the pane sees a drag, not a click. */
function selectText(text: string) {
  const node = screen.getByText(text).firstChild as Text;
  const range = document.createRange();
  range.setStart(node, 0);
  range.setEnd(node, text.length);
  const selection = window.getSelection()!;
  selection.removeAllRanges();
  selection.addRange(range);
}

afterEach(() => {
  window.getSelection()?.removeAllRanges();
});

const question = {
  qid: "q-26",
  qnum: 26,
  stimulus:
    "A recent poll showed that almost half of the city's residents believe that Mayor Walker is guilty of ethics violations.",
  questionStem:
    "Which one of the following, if true, most helps to explain the surprising fact stated above?",
  answerChoices: {
    A: "Almost all of the people who believe that Walker is guilty of ethics violations had thought his performance was poor.",
    B: "There has been an increase in ratings for Walker's political opponents.",
  },
};

function renderPane(questionOverride = question) {
  return render(
    <OfficialQuestionPane
      question={questionOverride}
      displayNumber={26}
      selectedAnswer="A"
      eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
      isFlagged={false}
      answerRevealed
      correctAnswer="A"
      highlights={[]}
      highlightMode="none"
      onAnswerSelect={vi.fn()}
      onEliminate={vi.fn()}
      onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
      onTextSelection={vi.fn()}
      onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
    />,
  );
}

While Explanation is on, all the student sees here is a\n * rule under the stimulus with a small chevron sitting on it, so she knows\n * there is more without being handed it. Pressing the chevron opens the style\n * pills (only when the source published more than one style) and the\n * labelled steps. The drawer's Explanation tab renders the same data.\n */\ninterface Props {\n  data?: LrExplanationData | null;\n  isLoading?: boolean;\n  isError?: boolean;\n  activeStyle: LrStyleKey | null;\n  onStyleChange: (style: LrStyleKey) => void;\n  fontScale?: number;\n  open: boolean;\n  onToggle: () => void;\n}\n\nexport default function ArgumentBreakdown({\n  data,\n  isLoading,\n  isError,\n  activeStyle,\n  onStyleChange,\n  fontScale = 1,\n  open,\n  onToggle,\n}: Props) {\n  const styles = data?.styles ?? [];\n  const { current, steps, borrowsStandard } = resolveBreakdownSteps(data, activeStyle);\n  const baseSize = 19 * fontScale;\n\n  let body: React.ReactNode = null;\n  if (open) {\n    if (isLoading) {\n      body = <p className=\"text-[13px] text-slate-400\">Loading explanation…</p>;\n    } else if (isError) {\n      body = (\n        <p className=\"text-[13px] text-slate-400\">\n          Could not load the explanation. Try again in a moment.\n        </p>\n      );\n    } else if (!data?.hasAuthored) {\n      body = (\n        <p className=\"text-[13px] leading-relaxed text-slate-500\">\n          No written explanation was published for this question.\n        </p>\n      );\n    } else {\n      body = (\n        <>\n          {styles.length > 1 && (\n            <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n              {styles.map((s) => {\n                const active = current != null && s.style === current.style;\n                return (\n                  <button\n                    key={s.style}\n                    type=\"button\"\n                    onClick={() => onStyleChange(s.style)}\n                    aria-pressed={active}\n                    className={cn(\n                      \"rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition-colors\",\n                      active\n                        ? \"bg-slate-900 text-white\"\n                        : \"border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100\",\n                    )}\n                  >\n                    {s.label}\n                  </button>\n                );\n              })}\n            </div>\n          )}\n          {borrowsStandard && (\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400\">same as Standard</p>\n          )}\n          {steps.map((step) => (\n            <div key={step.position}>\n              {/* Label on its own line, a step under a step, the way LSAT Lab\n                  sets Conclusion / Evidence / Evaluate / Goal. */}\n              <p\n                className=\"font-semibold text-slate-700\"\n                style={{ fontSize: `${baseSize * 0.85}px`, lineHeight: 1.4 }}\n              >\n                {step.label}\n              </p>\n              <p\n                className=\"mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-slate-800\"\n                style={{ fontSize: `${baseSize}px`, lineHeight: 1.6 }}\n              >\n                {step.body}\n              </p>\n            </div>\n          ))}\n        </>\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <section aria-label=\"Argument breakdown\" className=\"font-sans\">\n      {/* A rule with the expander sitting on it: the only thing on screen while closed. */}\n      <div className=\"relative flex items-center justify-center border-t border-slate-200\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={onToggle}\n          aria-expanded={open}\n          aria-label={open ? \"Hide argument breakdown\" : \"Show argument breakdown\"}\n          title={open ? \"Hide argument breakdown\" : \"Show argument breakdown\"}\n          className=\"-mt-[14px] flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-600 shadow-sm transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100\"\n        >\n          {open ? <ChevronUp size={16} /> : <ChevronDown size={16} />}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n      {open && <div className=\"mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4\">{body}</div>}\n    </section>\n  );\n}\n"}

{"file_path": "C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu-wt-explanation\\src\\components\\drill\\official\\InlineChoiceExplanation.tsx", "content": "import * as React from \"react\";\nimport { ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { cn } from \"@/lib/utils\";\n\n/**\n * The Choice Note: the per-choice half of a Question Explanation, sitting\n * directly beneath an answer choice in `OfficialQuestionPane`.\n *\n * The prose is a dropdown, CLOSED by default, behind a chevron at the right\n * edge of the choice card (`ChoiceNoteToggle`, rendered by the pane in its\n * icon column). LSAT Lab's shape: pressing Explanation shows the expanders,\n * and the student opens the notes she wants one at a time rather than having\n * five explanations pop down at once. Before 2026-09-12 the prose was always\n * visible; that read well on a shared screen but buried the choices under\n * five paragraphs the moment Explanation went on.\n *\n * Two rules carried over from the retired `ExplanationPanel`, applied per\n * choice:\n *  - Never invent content. A missing tag, percentage, or note renders as\n *    absent. A choice with no note gets no chevron and no dropdown at all,\n *    not an empty one, not a placeholder. The header row (tag, percentage,\n *    \"Your answer\") still renders when any of those exists.\n *  - The source's nuance lives in the tag chip verbatim (\"Too Weak\", \"Weakens,\n *    if anything\"). The prose heading only says which way the choice went.\n */\n\nexport interface InlineChoiceNote {\n  /** Wrong-answer type from the source, e.g. \"Out of Scope\". */\n  tag?: string | null;\n  /** Percent of students who picked this choice. */\n  studentPercent?: number | null;\n  /** The choice's explanation prose. Absent for letters the source skipped. */\n  note?: string | null;\n}\n\n/** Per-letter inline content for one question. A letter may legitimately be absent. */\nexport type InlineChoiceNotes = Record<string, InlineChoiceNote>;\n\ninterface Props {\n  letter: string;\n  /** Whether this letter is the correct answer: a fact, not a display state. */\n  isCorrect: boolean;\n  /** The student's own pick, marked so they can find it fast. */\n  isSelected: boolean;\n  content: InlineChoiceNote;\n  /** The dropdown is open. Only meaningful when the note has prose. */\n  open: boolean;\n}\n\n/** True when there is nothing real to render for this letter. */\nexport function isInlineChoiceEmpty(content: InlineChoiceNote | undefined, isSelected: boolean) {\n  if (isSelected) return false;\n  if (!content) return true;\n  return !content.tag && content.studentPercent == null && !content.note;\n}\n\n/** True when the note has prose worth a chevron. Whitespace is not prose. */\nexport function hasChoiceNoteProse(content: InlineChoiceNote | undefined): boolean {\n  return typeof content?.note === \"string\" && content.note.trim().length > 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * The expander at the right edge of the choice card. Lives in the pane's icon\n * column so it sits level with the choice text, exactly where LSAT Lab puts\n * it. Stops propagation: opening a note is never a pick.\n */\nexport function ChoiceNoteToggle({\n  letter,\n  open,\n  onToggle,\n  disabled,\n}: {\n  letter: string;\n  open: boolean;\n  onToggle: () => void;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n}) {\n  const label = open ? `Hide explanation for ${letter}` : `Show explanation for ${letter}`;\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      onClick={(event) => {\n        event.stopPropagation();\n        onToggle();\n      }}\n      disabled={disabled}\n      aria-expanded={open}\n      aria-label={label}\n      title={label}\n      className=\"mt-0.5 flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border border-slate-300 bg-white text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40\"\n    >\n      {open ? <ChevronUp size={16} /> : <ChevronDown size={16} />}\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n\nexport default function InlineChoiceExplanation({\n  letter,\n  isCorrect,\n  isSelected,\n  content,\n  open,\n}: Props) {\n  const { tag, studentPercent, note } = content;\n  const hasNote = hasChoiceNoteProse(content);\n\n  if (isInlineChoiceEmpty(content, isSelected)) return null;\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"mt-2.5\" data-choice-note={letter}>\n      {/* Tag, percentage, and the student's own pick. Any of these may be absent. */}\n      <div className=\"flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-[12px]\">\n        {tag && (\n          <span\n            className={cn(\n              \"rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold\",\n              isCorrect ? \"bg-green-600 text-white\" : \"bg-slate-200 text-slate-700\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {tag}\n          </span>\n        )}\n        {studentPercent != null && (\n          <span className=\"text-slate-500 tabular-nums\">{studentPercent}% of students</span>\n        )}\n        {isSelected && (\n          <span className=\"rounded-full border border-slate-300 px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500\">\n            Your answer\n          </span>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {/* No note means nothing here. The absence is the honest render. */}\n      {hasNote && open && (\n        <div\n          className={cn(\n            // A left rule in the choice's own colour anchors the prose to the\n            // choice it belongs to. Without it, five explanations down a page\n            // read as one undifferentiated column of text.\n            \"mt-2 border-l-2 pl-3\",\n            isCorrect ? \"border-green-600/50\" : \"border-slate-300\",\n          )}\n        >\n          <p\n            className={cn(\n              \"text-[12px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide\",\n              isCorrect ? \"text-green-800\" : \"text-slate-500\",\n            )}\n          >\n            {isCorrect ? \"Why this is correct\" : \"Why this is wrong\"}\n          </p>\n          {/* Reading size, not caption size: this is the sentence the student\n              is meant to actually read, and it carries the teaching. */}\n          <p className=\"mt-1 whitespace-pre-line text-[15px] leading-7 text-slate-800\">\n            {note}\n          </p>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"}

  const handleAnswerSelect = (answer: string) => {
    if (!isPracticeSetMode && !isRetryAfterWrong && answerLocked) return;
    if (!tutorMode && showSolution) return;
    
    if (selectedAnswer === answer) {
      setSelectedAnswer('');
      if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && currentQuestion) {
        const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
        newAttempts.delete(currentQuestion.qid);
        setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
        
        if (isPracticeSetMode) {
          questionTimer.current.recordAnswer(currentQuestion.qid, '');
        }
      }
    } else {
      setSelectedAnswer(answer);
      if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && currentQuestion) {
        const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
        const existingAttempt = newAttempts.get(currentQuestion.qid);
        // When this choice was made, measured from the start of the question.
        const atMs = Math.max(
          0,
          trailBaseMs.current + Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime),
        );
        const answerTrail = [...(existingAttempt?.answerTrail ?? []), { answer, atMs }];

        newAttempts.set(currentQuestion.qid, {
          selectedAnswer: answer,
          correct: answer === currentQuestion.correctAnswer,
          timeMs: isPracticeSetMode
            ? questionTimer.current.getTotalTime(currentQuestion.qid)
            : Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime),
          timestamp: Date.now(),
          confidence: existingAttempt?.confidence || null,
          reviewDone: false,
          answerRevealed: existingAttempt?.answerRevealed || false,
          answerTrail,
          // Every entry after the first is a change of mind. Derived here so
          // the count and the trail can never disagree.
          switchCount: Math.max(0, answerTrail.length - 1),
        });
        setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
        
        if (isPracticeSetMode) {
          questionTimer.current.recordAnswer(currentQuestion.qid, answer);
        }
      }
    }
  };

  const handleEliminateAnswer = (key: string) => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    const qid = currentQuestion.qid;
    setEliminatedByQid(prev => {
      const next = new Map(prev);
      const current = new Set(prev.get(qid) ?? []);
      if (current.has(key)) {
        current.delete(key);
      } else {
        current.add(key);
        if (selectedAnswer === key) {
          setSelectedAnswer('');
          if ((session?.mode === 'full-section' || isPracticeSetMode) && session) {
            const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
            newAttempts.delete(qid);
            setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
          }
        }
      }
      next.set(qid, current);
      return next;
    });
  };

  const persistAttempt = async (
    attemptData: Parameters<typeof saveAttemptToDatabase>[0],
  ): Promise<boolean> => {
    try {
      return await saveAttemptToDatabase(attemptData);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('[drill:persistAttempt] threw, surfacing as save-failed', {
        qid: attemptData.qid,
        mode: attemptData.mode,
        error: e,
      });
      return false;
    }
  };

  // Persists every selected-answer attempt in the session. Per design, this
  // is the gate before a section closes — every attempt MUST hit the DB or
  // we rethrow so handleFinishSection can surface the failure rather than
  // silently completing a section with missing data.
  //
  // timeMs values are checked: a value of 0 (or negative) indicates a broken
  // timer somewhere upstream and is logged + counted, never silently saved.
  const commitSessionAttempts = async (
    attempts: DrillSession['attempts'],
    mode: DrillMode
  ): Promise<{
    saved: number;
    wajFailed: number;
    timeMsZero: number;
    /** Attempt rows that landed, so a retry can skip them. */
    savedQids: string[];
    /** Journal rows that landed. A short list means closeout work is owed. */
----
  const handleSubmitNonAdaptive = async (
    answerOverride?: string,
    options?: { advanceAfterSubmit?: boolean },
  ) => {
    const ans = answerOverride ?? selectedAnswer;
    if (!currentQuestion || !ans || !session) return null;

    const correct = ans === currentQuestion.correctAnswer;
    const timeMs = Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime);

    // Save attempt to database (no confidence for non-adaptive)
    const saved = await persistAttempt({
      qid: currentQuestion.qid,
      correct,
      time_ms: timeMs,
      qtype: currentQuestion.qtype,
      level: currentQuestion.difficulty,
      confidence: null,
      mode: session.mode,
      selected_answer: ans,
    });
    if (!saved) {
      if (!classId && !user?.id) {
        toast.error("Your session is out of sync. Please refresh the page or sign back in.");
      } else {
        toast.error("Couldn't save your answer. Check your connection and try the next question.");
      }
    }
    await flushReplay(currentQuestion.qid, session.mode);
    if (currentViewId.current) QuestionPoolService.markViewAnswered(currentViewId.current);

    const newAttempts = new Map(session.attempts);
    newAttempts.set(currentQuestion.qid, {
      selectedAnswer: ans,
      correct,
      timeMs,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      confidence: null,
      reviewDone: false,
    });

    adaptiveEngine.recordAttempt({
      qid: currentQuestion.qid,
      correct,
      time_ms: timeMs,
      qtype: currentQuestion.qtype,
      difficulty: currentQuestion.difficulty,
      timestamp: new Date(),
      reasoningType: currentQuestion.reasoningType ?? [],
      questionFeatures: currentQuestion.questionFeatures ?? [],
    });

    // Track question usage
    if (classId && session?.mode) {
      QuestionPoolService.markQuestionSeen(currentQuestion.qid, classId, session.mode);
    }

    setSession({ ...session, attempts: newAttempts });
    if (!options?.advanceAfterSubmit) {
      setShowSolution(true);
    }
    return newAttempts;
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (options?: { advanceAfterSubmit?: boolean }) => {
    if (!currentQuestion || !selectedAnswer || !session) return;

    const correct = selectedAnswer === currentQuestion.correctAnswer;
    const timeMs = Math.floor(performance.now() - questionStartTime);

    // After wrong answer in adaptive mode, save attempt FIRST then show Joshua's feedback
    if (!correct && session.mode === 'adaptive') {
      // SAVE ATTEMPT FIRST
      const saved = await persistAttempt({
        qid: currentQuestion.qid,
        correct: false,
        time_ms: timeMs,
        qtype: currentQuestion.qtype,
        level: currentQuestion.difficulty,
        confidence: null,
        mode: session.mode,
        selected_answer: selectedAnswer,
      });
      if (!saved) {
        if (!classId && !user?.id) {
          toast.error("Your session is out of sync. Please refresh the page or sign back in.");
        } else {
          toast.error("Couldn't save your answer. Check your connection and try the next question.");
        }
        return;
      }

  const saveAttemptToDatabase = async (attemptData: {
    qid: string;
    correct: boolean;
    time_ms: number;
    qtype: string;
    level: number;
    confidence: number | null;
    mode: DrillMode;
    selected_answer?: string;
  }): Promise<boolean> => {
    const question = questionBank.getQuestion(attemptData.qid);
    if (!question) {
      throw new Error(`saveAttemptToDatabase: unknown qid ${attemptData.qid}`);
    }

    if (!classId && classIdLoading) {
      console.warn('saveAttemptToDatabase called before classId resolved, skipping');
      return false;
    }
    const resolvedClassId = classId || user?.id || '';
    if (!resolvedClassId) {
      throw new Error(
        `saveAttemptToDatabase: no class_id and no user.id for qid ${attemptData.qid}`,
      );
    }
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('attempts').insert({
        user_id: user?.id,
        class_id: resolvedClassId,
        qid: attemptData.qid,
        pt: question.pt,
        section: question.section,
        qnum: question.qnum,
        qtype: attemptData.qtype,
        level: attemptData.level,
        correct: attemptData.correct,
        time_ms: attemptData.time_ms,
        confidence: attemptData.confidence,
        mode: attemptData.mode,
        selected_answer: attemptData.selected_answer || null,
        timestamp_iso: new Date().toISOString(),
      });

      if (error) {
        console.error('[drill:saveAttempt] supabase insert failed', {
          userId: user?.id,
          qid: attemptData.qid,
          mode: attemptData.mode,
          error,
        });
        throw new Error(`saveAttemptToDatabase: insert failed (${error.message})`);
      }
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[drill:saveAttempt] threw', {
        userId: user?.id,
        qid: attemptData.qid,
        mode: attemptData.mode,
        err,
      });
      throw err;
    }
  };

  // UI-level helper for the per-question submit handlers. Wraps the throwing

  // logged at the service AND visible to the user via the caller's toast. This
  // does NOT silently swallow: failures are logged + surfaced through the
  // boolean return that callers already act on.

   * How much of the section clock has been spent, right now.

   * A countdown reports what is left, so the spend is the budget minus that; a

  const sectionElapsedMs = React.useCallback((): number | undefined => {
    if (!timer) return undefined;

  const openTutorForCurrent = (tab?: 'coach' | 'breakdown' | 'notes') => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    setTutorQuestionSnapshot(currentQuestion);
    setTutorUserAnswerSnapshot(selectedAnswer || '');
    setTutorChatMode(resolveTutorMode({
      userAnswer: selectedAnswer,
      answerRevealed: !!showAnswerRevealed.get(currentQuestion.qid),
    }));
    setTutorInitialTab(tab);
    setTutorChatOpen(true);
  };

  // Reveal the correct answer in place, without opening any panel. Mirrors the
  // auto-reveal toggle's effect for a student who left it off.
  const revealAnswerForCurrent = () => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => new Map(prev).set(currentQuestion.qid, true));
  };

  // Revealing is a toggle, not a one-way door. Pressing Answer or Explanation
  // again puts the question back the way it was: correct letter hidden, the
  // per-choice explanations collapsed, the choices live again. Teaching off a
  // built set needs exactly this — showing the answer used to spend the
  // question for good.
  //
  // Only the view is undone. An attempt already written keeps
  // `answerRevealed: true`, because that record is what happened, not what is
  // currently on screen.
  const hideAnswerForCurrent = () => {
    if (!currentQuestion) return;
    const qid = currentQuestion.qid;
    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => {
      if (!prev.has(qid)) return prev;
      const next = new Map(prev);
      next.delete(qid);
      return next;
    });
    // The peek is forgiven along with the reveal: `unscoredQids` only gates
    // this runner's chrome, so leaving it set would strand the question on
    // Next with no way back to Submit.
-----
      revealAnswerForCurrent();
      if (intent === 'explanation') openDrawerOn('explanation');
      return;
    }
    setPendingRevealIntent(intent);
    setRevealConfirmOpen(true);
  };

  const confirmUnscoredReveal = () => {
    if (currentQuestion) {
      setUnscoredQids((prev) => new Set(prev).add(currentQuestion.qid));
    }
    revealAnswerForCurrent();
    if (pendingRevealIntent === 'explanation') openDrawerOn('explanation');
    setRevealConfirmOpen(false);
  };

  // Answer and Explanation are the same switch with different reach. Answer
  // flips the reveal in place; Explanation flips it and takes the drawer with
  // it, so one press gives the whole breakdown and the next press clears the
  // screen back to an untouched question.
  const toggleAnswerReveal = (attempted: boolean, revealed: boolean) => {
    if (revealed) {
      hideAnswerForCurrent();
      return;
    }
    handleRevealRequest(attempted, 'answer');
  };

  const toggleExplanation = (
    attempted: boolean,
    revealed: boolean,
    explanationOpen: boolean,
  ) => {
    if (explanationOpen) {
      setNotesOpen(false);
      if (revealed) hideAnswerForCurrent();
      return;
    }
    if (revealed) {
      openDrawerOn('explanation');
      return;
    }
    handleRevealRequest(attempted, 'explanation');
  };




  const renderTutorOverlay = () => (
    <>
      {/* Floating Tutor Card — fixed bottom-left, rendered at page root so fixed positioning isn't clipped */}
      {tutorChatOpen && tutorQuestionSnapshot && (
        <ErrorBoundary
          onReset={() => {
            setTutorChatOpen(false);

          }}

          <TutorChatModal
            open={tutorChatOpen}

              flagged: isFlagged,

            }}
            onNext={() => {
              setTutorChatOpen(false);

              setTutorUserAnswerSnapshot('');
              setTutorChatMode('pre-answer');
              setAnswerLocked(false);
              const next = isPracticeSetMode ? handlePracticeSetNext : handleNext;
              next();
            }}

            question={{

            inlineNotes={explanationOpen ? inlineNotes : null}

            onTextSelection={handleTextSelection}
            onHighlightClick={handleHighlightClick}
          />
          {renderVoiceCoachChip()}
          {isAdaptiveMode ? (
            <OfficialSmartDrillBar
              autoReveal={autoReveal}
              onAutoRevealChange={handleAutoRevealChange}
              primaryAction={currentAttempted || currentUnscored ? 'next' : 'submit'}
              primaryDisabled={!selectedAnswer || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onSubmit={() => {
                void commitCurrentAdaptiveAnswer({
                  revealAfterSave: autoReveal,
                  advanceAfterSave: false,
                });
              }}
              onNext={() => setAdvanceToken(t => t + 1)}
              onSkip={() => {
                setSkippedQids(prev => new Set(prev).add(currentQuestion.qid));
                setAdvanceToken(t => t + 1);
              }}
              skipDisabled={currentAttempted || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onEnd={() => handleNavigation('/classroom')}
              disabled={!!timer?.isPaused}
            />
          ) : isFullSection ? (
            // A real timed section is not a submit-one-at-a-time drill: the
            // answer commits the moment it is selected, so Skip and Submit have
            // nothing to do. What the section needs is the official navigator —
            // the oval strip for the whole queue and prev/next arrows in the
            // bottom-right corner, exactly where the paper-and-LawHub interface
            // puts them. Leaving the section runs through Complete Section in
            // the header strip.
            <OfficialAnswerNavigator
              questionQueue={session.questionQueue}
              currentIndex={session.currentIndex}
              attemptedQids={attemptedQids}
              jumpEnabled={jumpEnabled}
              jumpDisabledReason={timer?.isPaused ? 'Paused — resume to navigate' : undefined}
              showQuestionList
              prevDisabled={session.currentIndex <= 0 || !!timer?.isPaused}
              nextDisabled={
                officialNextDisabled ||
                session.currentIndex >= session.questionQueue.length - 1
              }
              nextDisabledReason={
                timer?.isPaused
                  ? 'Paused — resume to navigate'
                  : session.currentIndex >= session.questionQueue.length - 1
                  ? 'Last question — use Complete Section to finish'
                  : undefined
              }
              onJumpTo={handleJumpTo}
              onPrev={officialPrev}
              onNext={() => { void officialNext(); }}
              revealEnabled={revealSealed ? false : sectionRevealEnabled}
              onRevealEnabledChange={revealSealed ? undefined : setSectionRevealEnabled}
            />
          ) : (
            // Same chrome as the adaptive Smart Drill, but driven by the fixed
            // queue the student hand-picked from the Smart LR Drill card. Submit
            // saves and reveals in place (honoring the auto-reveal toggle); Next
            // walks the queue via the shared officialNext; Skip advances past the
            // answer gate; End finishes the section.
            <OfficialSmartDrillBar
              autoReveal={autoReveal}
              onAutoRevealChange={handleAutoRevealChange}
              primaryAction={currentAttempted || currentUnscored ? 'next' : 'submit'}
              primaryDisabled={!selectedAnswer || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onSubmit={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  const submitted = selectedAnswer;
                  await handleSubmitNonAdaptive(submitted);
                  if (autoReveal) {
                    setShowAnswerRevealed(prev => new Map(prev).set(currentQuestion.qid, true));
                  }
                  // Pop the AI tutor (post-answer when revealed) on a wrong answer
                  // so the student gets a specific, back-and-forth explanation
                  // instead of the old dead "ANALYSIS VALID" placeholder.
                  const tutorLaunch = resolveTutorLaunch({
                    tutorModeEnabled: tutorMode,
                    correct: submitted === currentQuestion.correctAnswer,
                    answerRevealed: autoReveal,
                  });
                  if (tutorLaunch.shouldOpen) {
                    setAnswerLocked(true);
                    setTutorQuestionSnapshot(currentQuestion);
                    setTutorUserAnswerSnapshot(submitted);
                    setTutorChatMode(tutorLaunch.mode);
                    setTutorChatOpen(true);
                  }
                })();
              }}
              onNext={() => { void officialNext(); }}
              // A built set is a fixed list, so it walks both ways. Skip only
              // ever went forward, and only before the question was answered,
              // which left no way back to a question already worked — the one
              // thing reviewing a set is for.
              onPrev={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  await commitPendingSelection();
                  officialPrev();
                })();
              }}
              prevDisabled={session.currentIndex <= 0 || !!timer?.isPaused}
              onAdvance={() => {
                void (async () => {
                  await commitPendingSelection();
                  advanceInQueue();
                })();
              }}
              advanceDisabled={!!timer?.isPaused}
              // Submit is only ever the commit action now. Once the question is
              // answered (or deliberately revealed unscored) the Next pill is
              // what carries the student on, so a second "Next" in the primary
              // slot would just be the same control twice.
              primaryVisible={!currentAttempted && !currentUnscored}
              // A built set is a fixed, known list, so it navigates like a
              // section: every question gets an oval, and any of them can be
              // clicked. Jumping commits whatever is selected first, for the
              // same reason Previous does — leaving a question must never be
              // the thing that loses the answer on it.
              pager={
                <AnswerOvals
                  questionQueue={session.questionQueue}
                  currentIndex={session.currentIndex}
                  attemptedQids={attemptedQids}
                  jumpEnabled={!timer?.isPaused}
                  jumpDisabledReason={timer?.isPaused ? 'Paused — resume to navigate' : undefined}
                  onJumpTo={(index) => {
                    void (async () => {
                      await commitPendingSelection();
                      handleJumpTo(index);
                    })();
                  }}
                />
              }
              onEnd={() => handleNavigation('/classroom/drill')}
              disabled={!!timer?.isPaused}
            />
          )}
          </>
          )}
        </OfficialDrillFrame>
        {helpOpen && <HelpSheet open={helpOpen} onOpenChange={setHelpOpen} />}
        {renderVoiceCoachModal()}
        {renderTutorOverlay()}
      </>
    );
  }

    <>
      <AlertDialog open={showExitDialog} onOpenChange={setShowExitDialog}>

----
  const permissions = useUserPermissions();
  const { isLoading: qbLoading } = useQuestionBank();

  // Everything about the run itself comes from the Drill Run Spec, which the
  // gate in front of this page built from the launch in the address bar. The
  // runner does not parse the query string for it: one grammar, one reader.
  const state = useDrillRunSpec();
  const assignmentId = state.assignmentId ?? null;
  const guidedAssignmentId = state.guidedAssignmentId ?? null;
  const guidedDrillId = state.guidedDrillId ?? null;
  const guidedSetId = state.guidedSetId;
  const { set: guidedSet } = useHomeworkSet(guidedSetId);
  const [guidedInfoOpen, setGuidedInfoOpen] = React.useState(false);

  const practicingLabel = state.practicingLabel;

  // Context params for header/breadcrumbs (survive refresh)
  const origin = searchParams.get('origin');
  const returnTo = searchParams.get('returnTo');
  const drillReturnTo = state.returnTo ?? (returnTo && returnTo.startsWith('/') && !returnTo.startsWith('//') ? returnTo : undefined);
  // Which run and leg this section belongs to, when it belongs to one. Comes
  // from the launch rather than from returnTo: that string is a routing hint
  // and nothing load-bearing may depend on it. A refreshed leg used to lose
  // these and then had nothing to write its close-out against, so the section
  // could never be marked finished and the practice test stayed open forever.
  const ptRun = state.ptRun;
  // Routing and score-UI suppression only. Never a clock or integrity decision.
  const inSitting = isSittingReturn(drillReturnTo);
  /**
   * Answer and Explanation are not rendered at all for this run.
   *
   * Two runs are sealed. A practice-test sitting, because a sitting reveals
   * nothing until it is scored and the old per-question gate quietly broke
   * that: attempt a question inside a sitting and the reveal unlocked. And a
   * run launched `?sealed=1`, which is how an assigned section is handed over.
   *
   * The full-section gate further down is a different and weaker thing. It
   * withholds the reveal until the question is attempted and then allows it.
   * That is right for a section a student chose to sit; it is wrong for one
   * assigned as a measurement, where the answer to question 3 must not be
   * available while question 4 is still open.
   */
  const revealSealed = inSitting || !!state.sealed;
  const contextLabel = searchParams.get('label');
  const contextFocus = searchParams.get('focus');
  // Kept as a string: `?initialIndex=0` is a real request to open on the first
  // question, and a number would read as absent.

describe("OfficialQuestionPane", () => {
  it("keeps the revealed correct answer readable on the official white surface", () => {
    const { container } = renderPane();

    const root = container.firstElementChild;
    expect(root).toHaveClass("bg-white");
    expect(root).not.toHaveClass("bg-bg-shadow");

    // The choice text is not a button any more (a button cannot be
    // drag-selected, so it could not be highlighted); find it by its text.
    const correctAnswerText = choiceText(/Almost all of the people who believe that Walker is guilty/i);
    expect(correctAnswerText).toHaveClass("text-green-900");
    expect(correctAnswerText).not.toHaveClass("text-green-50");
  });

  it("renders multi-word speaker labels as separate dialogue turns", () => {
    const { container } = renderPane({
      ...question,
      stimulus:
        "Figorian Wildlife Commission: First speaker text.\n\nFigorian Development Commission: Second speaker text.",
    });

    expect(screen.getByText("Figorian Wildlife Commission:")).toHaveClass("font-bold");
    expect(screen.getByText("Figorian Development Commission:")).toHaveClass("font-bold");
    expect(container.querySelector(".space-y-4")?.children).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  it("does not treat a mid-argument clause ending in a colon as a speaker", () => {
    // Even if a stray break lands before a sentence clause, the renderer must not
    // bold it as a turn (regression: PT118 S3 Q7-8 showed three bold speakers).
    const { container } = renderPane({
      ...question,
      stimulus:
        "Figorian Wildlife Commission: First speaker text.\n\nTo protect wildlife we must regulate such development in Figoria: more text.\n\nFigorian Development Commission: Second speaker text.",
    });

    expect(
      screen.queryByText(
        "To protect wildlife we must regulate such development in Figoria:",
      ),
    ).not.toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(container.querySelector(".space-y-4")).not.toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});

describe("OfficialQuestionPane — revealing an answer", () => {
  function renderRevealed(props: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
    return render(
      <OfficialQuestionPane
        question={question}
        displayNumber={26}
        selectedAnswer="B"
        eliminatedAnswers={new Set()}
        isFlagged={false}
        answerRevealed
        correctAnswer="A"
        highlights={[]}
        highlightMode="none"
        onAnswerSelect={vi.fn()}
        onEliminate={vi.fn()}
        onToggleFlag={vi.fn()}
        onTextSelection={vi.fn()}
        onHighlightClick={vi.fn()}
        {...props}
      />,
    );
  }

  it("marks the choice they actually took, not only the one that was right", () => {
    renderRevealed();

    // B was chosen and is wrong; saying "A is correct" without saying "and you
    // took B" is only half of what a reveal is for.
    expect(choiceText(/There has been an increase in ratings/i)).toHaveClass("text-red-900");
  });

  it("does not leave the verdict to colour alone", () => {
    // ADR 0001: feedback must always carry an icon and a label, so a student who
    // cannot separate the green from the red still gets the answer.

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * db-pull.mjs — read a table out of the live database, and only read it.
 *
 * Read-only by construction: it speaks PostgREST over HTTP GET, so there is no
 * statement to get wrong and no way for this to write, drop or migrate
 * anything. That is the point. Arbitrary SQL against production is available
 * through the Lovable MCP connector, but that tool cannot distinguish a SELECT
 * from a DROP, so it stays gated and deliberately awkward. Pulling rows for an
 * audit should not require opening that door.
 *
 * Needs a service-role key, because RLS hides every row from the anon key and
 * an empty result is indistinguishable from an empty table. Set it in
 * ~/ai-os/.env (which is git-ignored and already holds every other credential):
 *
 *   SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY=<the service_role key from the Supabase dashboard,
 *                              Project Settings -> API. It is a long JWT
 *                              starting "eyJ", not the short anon key.>
 *
 * Usage:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts id,user_id,pt,section,qnum,selected_answer,correct
 *
 * Pairs with the grading auditor:
 *   node scripts/db-pull.mjs attempts > /tmp/attempts.json
 *   node scripts/audit-attempt-grading.mjs /tmp/attempts.json
 */
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');

function readEnv(file) {
  const out = {};
  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;
  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\n')) {
    const t = line.trim();
    if (!t || t.startsWith('#')) continue;
    const i = t.indexOf('=');
    if (i < 0) continue;
    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
  }
  return out;
}

const env = {
  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, '../../.env')),
  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, '.env')),
};

const table = process.argv[2];
const columns = process.argv[3] ?? '*';
if (!table) {
  console.error('usage: node scripts/db-pull.mjs <table> [columns]');
  process.exit(2);
}

const url = env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
const key = env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;

if (!url) {
  console.error('db-pull: VITE_SUPABASE_URL is not set in projects/lsatu/.env');
  process.exit(2);
}
if (!key) {
  console.error(
    'db-pull: SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is not set.\n' +
      '  Add it to ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored). Supabase dashboard -> Project Settings -> API\n' +
      '  -> service_role. Without it RLS returns zero rows and an empty table and a hidden\n' +
      '  one look identical, so this refuses to guess rather than reporting a false empty.',
  );
  process.exit(2);
}

// Page through: PostgREST caps a response, and a silently truncated pull is the
// failure mode that makes an audit say "all clear" over rows it never saw.
const PAGE = 1000;
const rows = [];
for (let from = 0; ; from += PAGE) {
  const res = await fetch(`${url}/rest/v1/${table}?select=${encodeURIComponent(columns)}`, {
    headers: {
      apikey: [REDACTED],
      Authorization: [REDACTED] ${key}`,
      Range: `${from}-${from + PAGE - 1}`,
      Prefer: 'count=exact',
    },
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    console.error(`db-pull: ${res.status} ${res.statusText}\n${(await res.text()).slice(0, 400)}`);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  const batch = await res.json();
  rows.push(...batch);
  if (batch.length < PAGE) break;
}

process.stderr.write(`db-pull: ${rows.length} row(s) from ${table}\n`);
process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({ rows }, null, 2));

Reasons are only what the data\n * proves:\n *   not ingested       no `lr_questions` row for the address\n *   no authored prose  a row exists, nothing passes the predicate\n * Nothing in the schema records a video, so there is no \"video only\" label;\n * the 14 known video-only questions (PT143, PT146 per the 2026-08-03\n * migration) are a subset of \"no authored prose\".\n *\n * Usage:\n *   node scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs\n *   node scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs --out /path/to/report.md\n *\n * Default output: ../../wiki/explanations/MISSING-EXPLANATIONS.md (the vault),\n * never the app repo.\n */\nimport fs from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { hasAuthoredProse } from './explanation-prose.mjs';\n\nconst __dirname = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst REPO = path.resolve(__dirname, '..');\n\nfunction readEnv(file) {\n  const out = {};\n  if (!fs.existsSync(file)) return out;\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8').split('\\n')) {\n    const t = line.trim();\n    if (!t || t.startsWith('#')) continue;\n    const i = t.indexOf('=');\n    if (i < 0) continue;\n    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^[\"']|[\"']$/g, '');\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nconst env = {\n  ...readEnv(path.resolve(REPO, '../../.env')),\n  ...readEnv(path.join(REPO, '.env')),\n};\n\nconst args = process.argv.slice(2);\nconst outIdx = args.indexOf('--out');\nconst OUT =\n  outIdx >= 0 && args[outIdx + 1]\n    ? path.resolve(args[outIdx + 1])\n    : path.resolve(REPO, '../../wiki/explanations/MISSING-EXPLANATIONS.md');\n\nconst url = env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;\nconst key = env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;\nif (!url) {\n  console.error('census: VITE_SUPABASE_URL is not set in projects/lsatu/.env');\n  process.exit(2);\n}\nif (!key) {\n  console.error(\n    'census: SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY is not set.\\n' +\n      '  Add it to ~/ai-os/.env (git-ignored).
