'';\n    const total = Number(range.split('/')[1]);\n    if (Number.isFinite(total)) expected = total;\n    const batch = await res.json();\n    rows.push(...batch);\n    if (batch.length < PAGE) break;\n  }\n  if (expected == null) throw new Error(`${table}: no exact count in Content-Range; refusing to trust the pull`);\n  if (rows.length !== expected) {\n    throw new Error(`${table}: pulled ${rows.length} rows but the server counts ${expected}`);\n  }\n  process.stderr.write(`census: ${rows.length} row(s) from ${table}\\n`);\n  return rows;\n}\n\n/** The bank: public/data/PT*-LR.json, one file per section. */\nfunction readBank() {\n  const dir = path.join(REPO, 'public', 'data');\n  const files = fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => /^PT\\d+-S\\d+-LR\\.json$/.test(f)).sort();\n  const questions = [];\n  for (const file of files) {\n    const rows = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir, file), 'utf8'));\n    for (const q of rows) {\n      questions.push({\n        pt: Number(q.practiceTest),\n        section: Number(q.sectionNumber),\n        qnum: Number(q.questionNumber),\n        qtype: q.questionType ?? '',\n      });\n    }\n  }\n  process.stderr.write(`census: ${questions.length} bank question(s) in ${files.length} file(s)\\n`);\n  return questions;\n}\n\nconst bank = readBank();\n\nlet questions;\nlet explanations;\nlet steps;\nlet notes;\ntry {\n  [questions, explanations, steps, notes] = await Promise.all([\n    pullAll('lr_questions', 'id,preptest,section,question_number'),\n    pullAll('lr_explanations', 'id,question_id,style'),\n    pullAll('lr_explanation_steps', 'explanation_id,body'),\n    pullAll('lr_explanation_choice_notes', 'explanation_id,body'),\n  ]);\n} catch (e) {\n  console.error(`census: aborted, nothing written.\\n${e instanceof Error ? e.message : e}`);\n  process.exit(1);\n}\n\nconst address = (pt, section, qnum) => `PT${pt}-S${section}-Q${qnum}`;\n\nconst stepsByExplanation = new Map();\nfor (const row of steps) {\n  if (!stepsByExplanation.has(row.explanation_id)) stepsByExplanation.set(row.explanation_id, []);\n  stepsByExplanation.get(row.explanation_id).push({ body: row.body });\n}\nconst notesByExplanation = new Map();\nfor (const row of notes) {\n  if (!notesByExplanation.has(row.explanation_id)) notesByExplanation.set(row.explanation_id, []);\n  notesByExplanation.get(row.explanation_id).push({ body: row.body });\n}\nconst stylesByQuestion = new Map();\nfor (const row of explanations) {\n  if (!stylesByQuestion.has(row.question_id)) stylesByQuestion.set(row.question_id, []);\n  stylesByQuestion.get(row.question_id).push({\n    steps: stepsByExplanation.get(row.id) ?? [],\n    notes: notesByExplanation.get(row.id) ?? [],\n  });\n}\nconst ingested = new Map();\nfor (const row of questions) {\n  ingested.set(address(row.preptest, row.section, row.question_number), row.id);\n}\n\nconst missing = [];\nlet withProse = 0;\nlet ingestedCount = 0;\nfor (const q of bank) {\n  const id = ingested.get(address(q.pt, q.section, q.qnum));\n  if (!id) {\n    missing.push({ ...q, reason: 'not ingested' });\n    continue;\n  }\n  ingestedCount += 1;\n  if (hasAuthoredProse(stylesByQuestion.get(id) ?? [])) {\n    withProse += 1;\n  } else {\n    missing.push({ ...q, reason: 'no authored prose' });\n  }\n}\nmissing.sort((a, b) => a.pt - b.pt || a.section - b.section || a.qnum - b.qnum);\n\nconst notIngested = missing.filter((m) => m.reason === 'not ingested').length;\nconst noProse = missing.filter((m) => m.reason === 'no authored prose').length;\nconst today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);\n\nconst lines = [];\nlines.push('# Questions with no authored explanation');\nlines.push('');\nlines.push(`_Generated ${today} by \\`scripts/missing-explanations-census.mjs\\` in the LSAT U repo. Tags and student percentages never count. Nothing in the schema records a video, so there is no video column here; the 14 known video-only questions (PT143, PT146, per the 2026-08-03 migration) fall under \"no authored prose\".');\nlines.push('');\n\nlet currentPt = null;\nfor (const m of missing) {\n  if (m.pt !== currentPt) {\n    currentPt = m.pt;\n    lines.push(`## PrepTest ${m.pt}`);\n    lines.push('');\n    lines.push('| PT | S | Q | Question type | Reason |');\n    lines.push('|---|---|---|---|---|');\n  }\n  lines.push(`| ${m.pt} | ${m.section} | ${m.qnum} | ${m.qtype} | ${m.reason} |`);\n  const next = missing[missing.indexOf(m) + 1];\n  if (!next || next.pt !== m.pt) lines.push('');\n}\n\nfs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(OUT), { recursive: true });\nfs.writeFileSync(OUT, lines.join('\\n'), 'utf8');\nprocess.stderr.write(`census: wrote ${missing.length} missing question(s) to ${OUT}\\n`);\n"}

export function DrillRunSpecProvider({
  spec,
  children,
}: {
  spec: DrillRunSpec;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <DrillRunSpecContext.Provider value={spec}>{children}</DrillRunSpecContext.Provider>
  );
}

/**
 * The run the student is in. An empty spec means the URL named no drill, which
 * the runner already handles by not starting one.
 */
export function useDrillRunSpec(): DrillRunSpec {
  return React.useContext(DrillRunSpecContext);
}

          const groups = new Map<string, LRQuestion[]>();
          
          for (const q of available) {
            const key = `${q.qtype}-${q.difficulty}`;
            if (!groups.has(key)) groups.set(key, []);
            groups.get(key)!.push(q);
          }
          
          // Shuffle each group
          for (const [, questions] of groups) {
            for (let i = questions.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
              const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
              [questions[i], questions[j]] = [questions[j], questions[i]];
            }
          }
          
          // Round-robin select
          const selected: LRQuestion[] = [];
          const groupArrays = Array.from(groups.values());
          
          while (selected.length < config.count && groupArrays.some(g => g.length > 0)) {
            for (const group of groupArrays) {
              if (group.length > 0 && selected.length < config.count) {
                selected.push(group.shift()!);

/** The stored run for this drill, or null when there is nothing to resume. */
export async function loadDrillResumeSession(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  legacyKey?: string | null,
): Promise<DrillResumeSession | null> {
  if (!userId || !key) return null;
  const current = await loadPracticeSession(userId, key);
  if (current || !legacyKey || legacyKey === key) return current;
  return loadPracticeSession(userId, legacyKey);
}

/** Write where the student is right now (local-first, debounced cloud upsert). */
export function saveDrillResumeSnapshot(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();
  return savePracticeSessionSnapshot(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);
}

/**
 * Mark the run finished. Nothing is deleted — the queue and every answer stay
 * in the row for review — it simply stops being offered as a resume, so the
 * next launch of the same drill draws fresh questions.
 */
export function completeDrillResumeSession(
  userId: string,
  key: string | null,
  snapshot: DrillResumeSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !key) return Promise.resolve();
  return completePracticeSession(userId, { slug: key, completionId: key }, snapshot);
}

/**
 * Is this stored row worth resuming?
 *
 * Unlike a guided drill, these runs are drawn fresh on every launch, so there
 * is no fixed set to compare against — the stored queue IS the run. It is
 * resumable when it is still in progress and the student actually answered
 * something; a row with no answers has nothing to restore and would only
 * freeze a stale shuffle in place.
 */
export function isResumableDrillSession(stored: DrillResumeSession | null): boolean {
  return (
    !!stored &&
    stored.status === 'in_progress' &&
    stored.question_queue.length > 0 &&
    stored.attempts.length > 0
  );

    [sectionElapsedMs],
  );

  /**
   * The clock keeps running between answers, and a student can sit on one
   * question for five minutes. Snapshots written only on answer changes would
   * hand those five minutes back on resume, so a timed section also saves on a
   * slow heartbeat. Held in a ref because the snapshot function changes on
   * every tick and would otherwise restart the interval each time.
   */
  const snapshotRef = React.useRef(drillResumeSnapshot);
  snapshotRef.current = drillResumeSnapshot;
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session || !timer) return;
    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.mode !== 'full-section') return;
    const id = window.setInterval(() => {
      const snapshot = snapshotRef.current(session);
      if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;
      void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
        console.error('[drill:resume:heartbeat] resume write failed', { err });
      });
    }, 20_000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [genericResumeKey, user, session, timer]);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!isMcPractice || !mcPracticePool || !user || !session) return;
    if (mcPracticeFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot) return;
    void savePracticeSessionSnapshot(user.id, mcPracticePool, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:mc-practice:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        slug: mcPracticePool.slug,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, isMcPractice, mcPracticePool, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

  // Same snapshot on every answer and every question move for guided drills, so
  // the student's place survives a tab close, a sign-out, or another device —
  // none of which a URL param can carry.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!guidedSessionKey || !user || !session) return;
    if (guidedFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot) return;
    void saveGuidedDrillSnapshot(user.id, guidedSessionKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:guided:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        key: guidedSessionKey,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, guidedSessionKey, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

  // And the same again for every other drill surface. Only runs with at least
  // one answered question are written: an untouched shuffle is not progress,
  // and storing it would freeze a stale question order in place.
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (!genericResumeKey || !user || !session) return;
    if (genericFinalizedRef.current) return;
    if (session.questionQueue.length === 0) return;
    const snapshot = drillResumeSnapshot(session);
    if (!snapshot || snapshot.attempts.length === 0) return;
    void saveDrillResumeSnapshot(user.id, genericResumeKey, snapshot).catch((err) => {
      // Non-fatal: the drill still works, they just lose the resume point.
      console.error('[drill:resume:snapshot] resume write failed', {
        userId: user.id,
        key: genericResumeKey,
        err,
      });
    });
  }, [session, genericResumeKey, user, drillResumeSnapshot]);

export function drillResumeKey(input: {
  mode: string;
  config?: unknown;
  identity?: DrillResumeIdentity;
}): string | null {
  if (input.identity?.kind === 'bootcamp-day') {
    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}bootcamp-day::${input.identity.day}::${input.identity.part}`;
  }

  const mode = input.mode;
  if (mode === 'adaptive') return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}adaptive`;

  const config = (input.config ?? null) as Record<string, unknown> | null;

  if (mode === 'full-section') {
    if (!config) return null;
    const pt = config.pt;
    const section = config.section;
    if (pt == null || section == null) return null;
    return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}section::pt${pt}-s${section}`;
  }

  if (mode === 'type-drill' || mode === 'natural-drill') {
    if (!config) return null;
    const selected = config.selectedQids as string[] | undefined;
    if (selected?.length) {
      // A pre-selected set is its own identity: the same list re-opened is the
      // same run, a different list is a different one.
      return `${DRILL_SESSION_PREFIX}${mode}::set-${fingerprint([...selected].sort().join(','))}`;
    }
    const shape = JSON.stringify({
      qtypes: config.qtypes ?? [],
      difficulties: config.difficulties ?? [],
      pts: config.pts ?? [],
      sections: config.sections ?? [],
      qfeatures: config.qfeatures ?? [],
      reasoningTypes: config.reasoningTypes ?? [],
      searchQuery: config.searchQuery ?? '',
      questionNumber: config.questionNumber ?? null,

/**
 * Write the student's current place in a practice set. Called after every
 * answer and every question move, so it must stay cheap — the persistence layer
 * writes to the local cache synchronously and debounces the Supabase upsert.
 *
 * A completed row is never downgraded back to in_progress by a snapshot write;
 * re-running a finished set starts a fresh row via resetPracticeSession.
 */
export async function savePracticeSessionSnapshot(
  userId: string,
  pool: Pick<PracticePool, 'slug' | 'completionId'>,
  snapshot: PracticeSessionSnapshot,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!userId || !pool?.slug) return;
  if (snapshot.questionQueue.length === 0) return;

  const id = practiceSessionIdFor(userId, pool.slug);
  const persist = getPersistence(userId);
  const existing = await loadPracticeSession(userId, pool.slug);

  const record = PracticeSession.parse({
    id,
    user_id: userId,
    pool_slug: pool.slug,
    completion_id: pool.completionId,
    question_queue: snapshot.questionQueue,
    current_index: Math.min(
      Math.max(snapshot.currentIndex, 0),
      Math.max(snapshot.questionQueue.length - 1, 0),
    ),
    attempts: snapshot.attempts,
    status: 'in_progress',
    // Never hand back time already spent: a snapshot with no clock keeps
    // whatever the row already knew rather than clearing it.
    elapsed_ms: snapshot.elapsedMs ?? existing?.elapsed_ms,
    regulation_answered_qids:
      snapshot.regulationAnsweredQids ?? existing?.regulation_answered_qids,
    started_at: existing?.started_at ?? now(),
    updated_at: now(),
  });

  await persist.set(TABLES.practice_sessions, id, record);
}

export interface Persistence {
  ready(): Promise<void>;

  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;
  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;
  remove(table: string, id: string): Promise<void>;
  list<T>(table: string, filter?: Partial<T>): Promise<T[]>;

  putBlob(key: string, blob: Blob): Promise<void>;
  getBlob(key: string): Promise<Blob | null>;
  removeBlob(key: string): Promise<void>;

  transaction<T>(fn: (tx: PersistenceTx) => Promise<T>): Promise<T>;
}

export interface PersistenceTx {
  get<T>(table: string, id: string): Promise<T | null>;
  set<T>(table: string, id: string, record: T): Promise<void>;
}
export function __setPersistenceForTests(p: Persistence | null): void {
  _testOverride = p;
  if (p === null) _cache.clear();
}

I
Necessary Assumption
THE UNSTATED BRIDGE

Sufficient Assumption

MATCH THE SHAPE

## user

Base directory for this skill: C:\Users\Joshu\.claude\skills\session-handoff

# Session Handoff

Produce a repeatable end-of-session summary so Joshua can `/clear` and start a fresh agent without losing continuity. The next agent should pick up by reading this summary alone.

This is a **context-handoff artifact**, not a status report. The audience is a future instance of you, not a stakeholder.

**Handoff vs. wrapup:** run `session-handoff` when the *context window* is the problem — nearing ~250k tokens (the dumb zone), or passing work between Claude and Codex. It is chat-only and writes nothing. Run `wrapup` when the *work day* is done — it strikes tasks, refreshes `wiki/hot.md`, and appends `wiki/log.md`. They can be run back to back (wrapup to persist, then handoff to reset).

## When to invoke

Joshua says: "session handoff", "hand off", "handoff summary", "summarize before I clear", or any near-equivalent. Note "let's wrap up" belongs to `wrapup`, not here — if he says that and the context window is also deep, run `wrapup` first, then offer this. Also invoke proactively if he says he's about to `/clear` without having run it, or if the session is clearly deep into the dumb zone.

## How to produce the summary

1. **Review the full conversation**, not just the last few turns. Handoffs miss things when they only summarize recent context.
2. **Pull state from these sources (in order):**
   - Plan files referenced this session (check `~/.claude/plans/` if a plan was mentioned).
   - TodoWrite state — any in-progress or pending tasks.
   - Background processes started with `run_in_background` — shell IDs are load-bearing for the next agent.
   - Files created or modified this session — you know what you touched; don't grep to re-discover.
   - Memory files written or updated (`~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/`).
   - Unresolved questions — things you asked Joshua that never got a clear answer, or things he asked that got deflected.
3. **Do NOT audit the filesystem.** This is synthesis of what happened in THIS session. No `git log`, no broad `Glob` sweeps. If you didn't touch it this session, it doesn't belong here.
4. **Produce the output in chat.** Do not write a file. Do not update memory. Chat-only.

## Output template — use exactly this structure, every time

```
# Session Handoff — <one-line title of what this session was about>

## Where it started
<2-3 sentences: what Joshua asked for, key framing or constraints that emerged>

## Decisions locked + what shipped
- <decision or change> — <why, and where it lives (absolute path if a file)>
- ...

## Key files for next session
- `<absolute path>` — <why the next agent should read this first>
- Plan file: `<path>` (if a plan drove the session)
- Memory files touched: `<paths>` (if any)

## Running state
- Background processes: <shell IDs + what they are + how to kill> — or "none"
- Dev servers / ports: <url + port> — or "none"
- Open worktrees / branches: <paths> — or "none"

## Verified state as of <HH:MM>
- <claim about live/system state> — <how it was checked, and when>
- <claim> — NOT re-verified this session; last confirmed <when>
- ...

## Verification — how to confirm things still work
- `<command>` — <expected outcome>
- ...

## Deferred + open questions
- Deferred: <item> — <why pushed to later>
- OPEN (must be resolved or re-asked before new work): <question needing Joshua's input> — <context>

## Pick up here
<1-2 sentences: the single most likely next action for a fresh agent>
```

## Hard rules

1. **Chat output only.** Never write the handoff to a file. Never update memory from this skill. (That is `wrapup`'s job, not this one.)
2. **Never invent state.** If a section has nothing to report, write "none" — do not omit the section. Structure stability is the whole point.
3. **Absolute paths always.** The next agent may have a different working directory, or be Codex.
4. **If a plan file drove the session, name it first** in "Key files" so the next agent reads it before anything else.
5. **No emojis, no hype, no "great job" summaries.** Terse and concrete — paths, commands, shell IDs, decisions. Tone of a seasoned engineer handing off at end-of-shift.
6. **Background process IDs are critical.** If you started any `run_in_background` shells, their IDs must appear in "Running state" with the kill command — the next agent cannot find them otherwise.
7. **Stamp verified state, and separate it from assumed state.** Run `date "+%H:%M"` and use the real time. Every claim about live systems (deploy status, DB contents, what was sent, what is published) is either something you actually checked this session — say how — or explicitly marked NOT re-verified. A handoff that presents stale belief as current state makes the next session re-derive everything, which is the exact cost this skill exists to remove.
8. **Open questions are obligations, not notes.** Anything under OPEN must be resolved by the next session or re-asked to Joshua before it starts new work. Requirements have been silently re-typed across sessions, and a promised artifact once never arrived because nobody re-asked. If a question was open in the handoff you are picking up from, it is the first thing you raise.

## Anti-patterns — do not do these

- Summarizing the last 3 turns and calling it a handoff.
- Listing files by relative path.
- Skipping "Running state" because "nothing is running" — write "none" instead.
- Writing the summary to any file. This is chat-only by design.
- Adding a "what went well / what went poorly" retrospective. This isn't a retro.
- Recommending next steps beyond the single "Pick up here" line. The next agent decides; you just hand off.
